+### [2026-11-28 21:16] Version 1.8.200 — Unified Youth & Kids Admission Success Popup, Clean UTF-8, Fixed Kids HO Receipt Code & Mobile Modal Overflow Fixes
+
+**What was built:**
+- **Unified New Admission Success Popup (`public/js/components/payment-popup.js`, `public/js/pages/admissions.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js`)**:
+  - Removed `promptKidsAdmissionSuccess` (4 buttons) and replaced both Youth and Kids post-admission modals with ONE shared `promptAdmissionSuccess` popup (`"Admission Successful!"` with green animated checkmark and summary box).
+  - For Youth Paid, Kids Paid, and Kids No-payment admissions, the receipt / admission slip preview (`#/admissions/<id>/receipt` or `#/school-admissions/<id>/receipt`) now opens behind the popup automatically.
+  - Displays **3 icon buttons in order**: `Print receipt` (or `Print slip` for Kids no payment — waits until the background sheet finishes rendering, closes modal, calls `window.print()`), `Admit another` (navigates to `#/students?new=1` or `#/school-students?new=1` to open a blank admission form), and `Done` (closes the popup, revealing the receipt/slip behind it).
+  - Kept **"Payment Recorded!"** (`promptOpenReceipt`: `Open Receipt`, `Print`, `Done`) unchanged for monthly fee / installment / upgrade payments, and kept Kids `"Confirm admission"` popup before No-payment admissions. Added `?new=1` form-open support in `school-admissions.page.js`.
+- **Clean UTF-8 Across Project (`public/js/components/ui-datepicker.js` + `11` Kids `public/js/**` files)**:
+  - Replaced raw Windows-1252 `0x97` byte in `ui-datepicker.js` and all `U+FFFD` replacement characters across `12` source files (including `"New admission · not submitted"` / `"Existing admission · payment unchanged"`, subtitle `·`, and table placeholders `—`) with valid UTF-8 characters. Verified `0` BOMs, `0` invalid UTF-8 files, and `0` mojibake across the repo.
+- **Fixed Kids Receipt Centre Code (`HO`) (`public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js`, `public/js/utils/school-billing.js`, `public/pages/org-details.html`, `public/js/pages/org-details.page.js`)**:
+  - Removed live overrides from `settings/org_config.receiptCode` (which caused new slips like `UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005` when stale test data `'UKKKKK'` sat in `org_config`). New Kids admission (`UK<YY>AD-HO-<NNNN>`), monthly (`UK<YY>MN-HO-<NNNN>`), and upgrade (`UK<YY>UP-HO-<NNNN>`) receipts now always use Kids' Head Office code `'HO'`. Existing receipt numbers in Firestore remain untouched. Set `#org-receiptCode` in `#/org` to read-only `'HO'`.
+- **Popup Overflow Fixes Across `360px`, `768px`, `1440px` (`public/css/components/list-page.css`, `public/css/pages/pay-fees.css`, `public/css/pages/student-profile.css`)**:
+  - Made `.pf-success-modal` scrollable within viewport (`max-height: min(92dvh, calc(100vh - 24px))`, `overflow-y: auto`), stacked `.pf-success-actions` and `.adm-view-foot` buttons cleanly on narrow mobile (`<= 480px`), collapsed `.sp-pay-summary-box` to 1 column at `<= 480px`, wrapped `.pk-current-badge` & `.pf-success-val`, and compacted mobile modal headers/bodies (`<= 480px`).
+
+**Why:**
+- Deliver one consistent admission confirmation flow across Youth and Kids over the live receipt preview, eliminate encoding artifacts (`U+FFFD` / `0x97`), stop stale `org_config.receiptCode` test values (`UKKKKK`) from corrupting new Kids receipt numbers, and ensure zero button/grid overflow down to `360px` viewports.
+

+- MODIFIED: `public/js/components/payment-popup.js`, `public/js/pages/admissions.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js`, `public/js/utils/school-billing.js`, `public/pages/org-details.html`, `public/js/pages/org-details.page.js`, `public/js/components/ui-datepicker.js`, `public/js/pages/school-admission-receipt.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-delete.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-end.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-fees.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-students.page.js`, `public/js/pages/school-transfer.page.js`, `public/js/utils/school-lifecycle.js`, `public/js/utils/school-receipt-pdf.js`, `public/js/utils/school-receipt.js`, `public/css/components/list-page.css`, `public/css/pages/pay-fees.css`, `public/css/pages/student-profile.css`, `docs/02-PROGRESS-LOG.md` (+ `bump-version.js 1.8.200` across `55` files in `public/`)

++# Release v1.8.200 Review Report — Unified Admission Popup, UTF-8 Cleanup, Fixed Kids HO Code & Mobile Overflow Fixes
++
++**Scope:** Shared Payment / Success Popups (`payment-popup.js`, Youth + Kids), Admissions (`admissions.page.js`, `school-admissions.page.js`), Kids Billing & Receipts (`school-billing.js`, `org-details.html`, `org-details.page.js`), UTF-8 cleanup (`12` JS files), and Shared Modal CSS (`list-page.css`, `pay-fees.css`, `student-profile.css`).  
++**Base commit compared:** `0b424b4` (`v1.8.199`)  
++**Plain text diff file:** `docs/reports/v1.8.200-changes.txt`  
++**Deploy status:** **Not deployed yet** — committed & pushed to `main`; tested strictly on local Firebase Emulators + synthetic test data.
++
++---
++
++## 1. What Went Wrong With Kids Receipt Numbers (`UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005`)
++
++- **Youth Centre** generates receipt numbers from each branch's own short `code` (for example `BYCTC` -> `UI26AD-BYCTC-0001`).
++- **Unique Kids** has only **one** school, co-located at **Head Office** (`branchId: 'HO'`, `branchCode: 'HO'`). However, back in `v1.8.69`, an editable **Receipt code (Kids)** input (`settings/org_config.receiptCode`) was added to `#/org` (accepting any 2–12 letters) and `school-admissions.page.js` + `school-billing.js` used `org_config.receiptCode` **ahead of** the built-in `'HO'` code (`centreCode = configuredCode || 'HO'`).
++- When `'UKKKKK'` was saved into `settings/org_config.receiptCode` in the live database during testing/setup, every subsequent new Kids receipt pulled `'UKKKKK'` into the centre segment (`UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005`).
++- **Fix in `v1.8.200`:**
++  - All **new** Kids receipts in [school-admissions.page.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js) (`AD`), `recordKidsBillPayment` (`MN`), and `upgradeKidsPackage` (`UP`) in [school-billing.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/utils/school-billing.js) now always use Kids' Head Office code **`HO`** (`UK26AD-HO-0001`, `UK26MN-HO-0001`, `UK26UP-HO-0001`), ignoring any stale value in `settings/org_config.receiptCode`.
++  - `#org-receiptCode` in [org-details.html](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/pages/org-details.html) & [org-details.page.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/pages/org-details.page.js) is now read-only **`HO`** so admins cannot accidentally misconfigure it, and opening `#/org` does not trigger a false unsaved-changes state.
++  - **Existing receipts untouched:** Previously saved receipt numbers (including `UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005`) are preserved as-is in Firestore and continue to render normally via [school-receipt.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/utils/school-receipt.js).
++
++---
++
++## 2. All Other Changes (Plain English)
++
++1. **ONE Shared Admission Success Popup (`promptAdmissionSuccess`) for Youth & Kids**
++   - Removed the separate 4-button `promptKidsAdmissionSuccess` modal in [payment-popup.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/components/payment-popup.js) and replaced it with ONE shared `promptAdmissionSuccess` popup used after **Youth Paid**, **Kids Paid**, and **Kids No-payment** admissions.
++   - Immediately upon admission save, the receipt / admission slip route (`#/admissions/<id>/receipt` or `#/school-admissions/<id>/receipt`) opens **behind the popup**.
++   - **Summary box:**
++     - **Paid (Youth & Kids):** Receipt No., Student, Fee Head / Level, Amount Paid, Payment Mode (plus optional TXN ID), Date.
++     - **Kids No payment:** Slip No., Student, Level, `₹0 (No payment) – <reason>`, `Monthly ₹X from <month>`, Date (no Payment Mode row).
++   - **Exactly 3 icon buttons in order:**
++     1. **Print receipt** (`Print slip` for Kids no payment) with printer icon -> closes popup, waits until the background receipt/slip renders cleanly in `#view`, then calls `window.print()`.
++     2. **Admit another** (`user-plus` icon) -> closes popup and opens a fresh blank admission form (`#/students?new=1` or `#/school-students?new=1`; added `?new=1` support in [school-admissions.page.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js)).
++     3. **Done** (`check` icon) -> closes popup only, revealing the receipt / slip preview behind it.
++   - Monthly fee payments, installments, and Kids Level Upgrades keep **"Payment Recorded!"** (`Open Receipt`, `Print`, `Done`) unchanged. Kids **"Confirm admission"** popup before No-payment admissions is also kept.
++
++2. **Fixed All Broken Characters (``) & Encoding Across the Whole Repo**
++   - Replaced raw Windows-1252 byte `0x97` in [ui-datepicker.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/components/ui-datepicker.js) and every `\uFFFD` (``) across 12 source files (`school-admissions.page.js` including `"New admission · not submitted"`, `"Existing admission · payment unchanged"`, `·` separators, and `—` placeholders; plus file headers/comments in 11 other Kids & receipt modules) with clean UTF-8 without BOM.
++   - Verified `hasBom: []`, `invalidUtf8: []`, and `mojibake: []` across all `.html`, `.js`, `.css`, `.md`, and `.json` files in the project (excluding literal test-check mentions in historical progress-log lines).
++
++3. **Responsive Popup Overflow Fixes (`360px`, `768px`, Desktop)**
++   - Updated [list-page.css](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/css/components/list-page.css), [pay-fees.css](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/css/pages/pay-fees.css), and [student-profile.css](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/css/pages/student-profile.css):
++     - Added viewport max-height & vertical scroll (`max-height: min(92dvh, calc(100vh - 24px))`, `overflow-y: auto; overflow-x: hidden;`) on `.pf-success-modal`.
++     - Stacked `.pf-success-actions` and `.adm-view-foot` buttons full-width cleanly on narrow screens (`<= 480px`) and allowed wrapping cleanly at medium widths.
++     - Switched `.sp-pay-summary-box` from 3 columns to 1 column at `<= 480px`.
++     - Compacted `.adm-view-head` / `.adm-view-body` mobile padding and added `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on `.pk-current-badge` & `.pf-success-val`.
++

++## 5. Unique Kids vs Youth Centre — Parity Comparison
++
++| Feature / Area | Youth Centre | Unique Kids | Parity Status in `v1.8.200` |
++| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
++| New admission success popup | `"Admission Successful!"` over background receipt (`Print receipt`, `Admit another`, `Done`) | `"Admission Successful!"` over background slip (`Print receipt`/`Print slip`, `Admit another`, `Done`) | **Full Parity (`v1.8.200`)** |

++| Receipt centre code | Uses branch code (e.g. `UI26AD-BYCTC-0001`) | Uses Head Office `'HO'` (e.g. `UK26AD-HO-0001`) | **Consistent per branch structure (`v1.8.200`)** |

+# Release v1.8.200 Review Report — Unified Admission Popup, UTF-8 Cleanup, Fixed Kids HO Code & Mobile Overflow Fixes
+
+**Scope:** Shared Payment / Success Popups (`payment-popup.js`, Youth + Kids), Admissions (`admissions.page.js`, `school-admissions.page.js`), Kids Billing & Receipts (`school-billing.js`, `org-details.html`, `org-details.page.js`), UTF-8 cleanup (`12` JS files), and Shared Modal CSS (`list-page.css`, `pay-fees.css`, `student-profile.css`).  
+**Base commit compared:** `0b424b4` (`v1.8.199`)  
+**Plain text diff file:** `docs/reports/v1.8.200-changes.txt`  
+**Deploy status:** **Not deployed yet** — committed & pushed to `main`; tested strictly on local Firebase Emulators + synthetic test data.
+
+---
+
+## 1. What Went Wrong With Kids Receipt Numbers (`UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005`)
+
+- **Youth Centre** generates receipt numbers from each branch's own short `code` (for example `BYCTC` -> `UI26AD-BYCTC-0001`).
+- **Unique Kids** has only **one** school, co-located at **Head Office** (`branchId: 'HO'`, `branchCode: 'HO'`). However, back in `v1.8.69`, an editable **Receipt code (Kids)** input (`settings/org_config.receiptCode`) was added to `#/org` (accepting any 2–12 letters) and `school-admissions.page.js` + `school-billing.js` used `org_config.receiptCode` **ahead of** the built-in `'HO'` code (`centreCode = configuredCode || 'HO'`).
+- When `'UKKKKK'` was saved into `settings/org_config.receiptCode` in the live database during testing/setup, every subsequent new Kids receipt pulled `'UKKKKK'` into the centre segment (`UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005`).
+- **Fix in `v1.8.200`:**
+  - All **new** Kids receipts in [school-admissions.page.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js) (`AD`), `recordKidsBillPayment` (`MN`), and `upgradeKidsPackage` (`UP`) in [school-billing.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/utils/school-billing.js) now always use Kids' Head Office code **`HO`** (`UK26AD-HO-0001`, `UK26MN-HO-0001`, `UK26UP-HO-0001`), ignoring any stale value in `settings/org_config.receiptCode`.
+  - `#org-receiptCode` in [org-details.html](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/pages/org-details.html) & [org-details.page.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/pages/org-details.page.js) is now read-only **`HO`** so admins cannot accidentally misconfigure it, and opening `#/org` does not trigger a false unsaved-changes state.
+  - **Existing receipts untouched:** Previously saved receipt numbers (including `UK26AD-UKKKKK-0005`) are preserved as-is in Firestore and continue to render normally via [school-receipt.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/utils/school-receipt.js).
+
+---
+
+## 2. All Other Changes (Plain English)
+
+1. **ONE Shared Admission Success Popup (`promptAdmissionSuccess`) for Youth & Kids**
+   - Removed the separate 4-button `promptKidsAdmissionSuccess` modal in [payment-popup.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/components/payment-popup.js) and replaced it with ONE shared `promptAdmissionSuccess` popup used after **Youth Paid**, **Kids Paid**, and **Kids No-payment** admissions.
+   - Immediately upon admission save, the receipt / admission slip route (`#/admissions/<id>/receipt` or `#/school-admissions/<id>/receipt`) opens **behind the popup**.
+   - **Summary box:**
+     - **Paid (Youth & Kids):** Receipt No., Student, Fee Head / Level, Amount Paid, Payment Mode (plus optional TXN ID), Date.
+     - **Kids No payment:** Slip No., Student, Level, `₹0 (No payment) – <reason>`, `Monthly ₹X from <month>`, Date (no Payment Mode row).
+   - **Exactly 3 icon buttons in order:**
+     1. **Print receipt** (`Print slip` for Kids no payment) with printer icon -> closes popup, waits until the background receipt/slip renders cleanly in `#view`, then calls `window.print()`.
+     2. **Admit another** (`user-plus` icon) -> closes popup and opens a fresh blank admission form (`#/students?new=1` or `#/school-students?new=1`; added `?new=1` support in [school-admissions.page.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/pages/school-admissions.page.js)).
+     3. **Done** (`check` icon) -> closes popup only, revealing the receipt / slip preview behind it.
+   - Monthly fee payments, installments, and Kids Level Upgrades keep **"Payment Recorded!"** (`Open Receipt`, `Print`, `Done`) unchanged. Kids **"Confirm admission"** popup before No-payment admissions is also kept.
+
+2. **Fixed All Broken Characters (`U+FFFD`) & Encoding Across the Whole Repo**
+   - Replaced raw Windows-1252 byte `0x97` in [ui-datepicker.js](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/js/components/ui-datepicker.js) and every `U+FFFD` replacement character across 12 source files (`school-admissions.page.js` including `"New admission · not submitted"`, `"Existing admission · payment unchanged"`, `·` separators, and `—` placeholders; plus file headers/comments in 11 other Kids & receipt modules) with clean UTF-8 without BOM.
+   - Verified `hasBom: []`, `invalidUtf8: []`, `hasFFFD: []`, and zero mojibake across all `189` first-party `.html`, `.js`, `.css`, `.md`, and `.json` files (excluding third-party `public/vendor/*` and historical grep-example lines `2354–3239` in `docs/02-PROGRESS-LOG.md`).
+
+3. **Responsive Popup Overflow Fixes (`360px`, `768px`, Desktop)**
+   - Updated [list-page.css](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/css/components/list-page.css), [pay-fees.css](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/css/pages/pay-fees.css), and [student-profile.css](file:///C:/Users/Asus/WebstormProjects/unique-infotech-cms/public/css/pages/student-profile.css):
+     - Added viewport max-height & vertical scroll (`max-height: min(92dvh, calc(100vh - 24px))`, `overflow-y: auto; overflow-x: hidden;`) on `.pf-success-modal`.
+     - Stacked `.pf-success-actions` and `.adm-view-foot` buttons full-width cleanly on narrow screens (`<= 480px`) and allowed wrapping cleanly at medium widths.
+     - Switched `.sp-pay-summary-box` from 3 columns to 1 column at `<= 480px`.
+     - Compacted `.adm-view-head` / `.adm-view-body` mobile padding and added `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on `.pk-current-badge` & `.pf-success-val`.
+

+## 5. Unique Kids vs Youth Centre — Parity Comparison
+
+| Feature / Area | Youth Centre | Unique Kids | Parity Status in `v1.8.200` |
+| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
+| New admission success popup | `"Admission Successful!"` over background receipt (`Print receipt`, `Admit another`, `Done`) | `"Admission Successful!"` over background slip (`Print receipt`/`Print slip`, `Admit another`, `Done`) | **Full Parity (`v1.8.200`)** |
+| `?new=1` admit-another route | `#/students?new=1` opens fresh blank form | `#/school-students?new=1` opens fresh blank form | **Full Parity (`v1.8.200`)** |
+| Receipt centre code | Uses branch code (e.g. `UI26AD-BYCTC-0001`) | Uses Head Office `'HO'` (e.g. `UK26AD-HO-0001`) | **Consistent per branch structure (`v1.8.200`)** |
+| Non-cash TXN ID rule | Optional (`(Optional)`), Cash disabled | Optional (`(Optional)`), Cash disabled | **Matched** |
+| Post-admission confirmation | Verify dialog (`Admit & Proceed to Payment`) + `openPaymentPopup` | `openPaymentPopup` (Paid) or `"Confirm admission"` (`No payment`) | **Matched / Covered** |
+| Monthly / Upgrade fee popup | `"Payment Recorded!"` (`Open Receipt`, `Print`, `Done`) | `"Payment Recorded!"` (`Open Receipt`, `Print`, `Done`) | **Matched** |
+| Profile Documents tab | `4` docs (`DOB Proof`, `Marksheet`, `Photo`, `Signature`) + extras | Strictly `2` docs (`Passport Photo`, `Student Signature`) | **Intentional difference per Kids rule** |
+| Reports & Offline Archives | `#/reports` & `#/archives` | Not enabled for Kids (`youth` only in `router.js`) | **Missing in Kids (unchanged by design)** |
+| Inline profile Transfer / End | Inline modals inside Student Profile | Dedicated `#/school-transfer` & `#/school-end` pages | **Missing inline profile modals in Kids** |
+

+- Live Firebase Hosting deploy (`firebase deploy --only hosting`) has **not** been run yet (waiting for your approval).
